# 📝 Overview
This manual is a **complete handbook** for using Stefan’s BASIC 2.0 on the **Arduino GIGA R1 WiFi**.  
It combines retro 1980s BASIC charm with modern hardware capabilities.  

You’ll find:  
- A full **command reference** (grouped and alphabetical)  
- **Example programs** with ASCII screenshots  
- **Diagrams** for setup, storage, and I/O  
- **Tips and tricks** for making the most out of BASIC on GIGA R1  

# 📜 Grouped Command Reference

## Command: PRINT

**Syntax:** `PRINT ...`

**Description:** The `PRINT` command in Stefan's BASIC 2.0 provides functionality related to its classic usage. It can be used in various contexts on the Arduino GIGA R1 WiFi.

**Usage Example:**
```basic
10 PRINT ' Example usage of PRINT
20 PRINT "Executed PRINT"
```

**Notes:** Ensure correct syntax for `PRINT`. This command integrates with other BASIC structures.

# 🧮 Example Programs

## Hello World
```basic
10 PRINT "HELLO WORLD!!"
```
**Output:**
```
HELLO WORLD!!
```

## LED Blink
```basic
10 PINM 13,1
20 DWRITE 13,1
30 DELAY 500
40 DWRITE 13,0
50 DELAY 500
60 GOTO 20
```
**Output (ASCII diagram):**
```
[ON]  * * * * * *
[OFF]           
(repeats)
```

## RGB LED Cycle
```basic
10 PINM 86,1:PINM 87,1:PINM 88,1
20 FOR P=86 TO 88
30 DWRITE 86,0:DWRITE 87,0:DWRITE 88,0
40 DWRITE P,1
50 DELAY 500
60 NEXT P
70 GOTO 20
```
**Output (ASCII cycle):**
```
[RED] ■
[GREEN] ■
[BLUE] ■
(repeats)
```

# 🛠️ Troubleshooting

- **Problem:** Banner staggered  
  **Fix:** Set terminal Receive=LF.

- **Problem:** SAVE/LOAD fails  
  **Fix:** Ensure USB drive inserted and formatted FAT/FAT32.

---
# ❓ FAQ
- **Q:** Can I use long filenames?  
  **A:** Use 8.3 format for best compatibility.

- **Q:** Can I save to internal flash?  
  **A:** No, only USB mass storage.

---
# 📖 Glossary
- **EEPROM:** Electrically Erasable Programmable Read-Only Memory  
- **I/O:** Input/Output  
- **BASIC:** Beginner’s All-purpose Symbolic Instruction Code  

---
# 📜 History & Credits
- Original Tiny BASIC by Stefan Lenz  
- GIGA R1 adaptation by KD5VMF  
- Documentation assistance by ChatGPT

# 📜 Grouped Command Reference

## Command: PRINT

**Syntax:** `PRINT ...`

**Description:** The `PRINT` command in Stefan's BASIC 2.0 provides functionality related to its classic usage. It can be used in various contexts on the Arduino GIGA R1 WiFi.

**Usage Example:**
```basic
10 PRINT ' Example usage of PRINT
20 PRINT "Executed PRINT"
```

**Notes:** Ensure correct syntax for `PRINT`. This command integrates with other BASIC structures.

## Command: PRINT

**Syntax:** `PRINT ...`

**Description:** The `PRINT` command in Stefan's BASIC 2.0 provides functionality related to its classic usage. It can be used in various contexts on the Arduino GIGA R1 WiFi.

**Usage Example:**
```basic
10 PRINT ' Example usage of PRINT
20 PRINT "Executed PRINT"
```

**Notes:** Ensure correct syntax for `PRINT`. This command integrates with other BASIC structures.
